### 🐒 Regarding "¿vergas vergas vergas… a Donde?"

The phrase "¿vergas vergas vergas… a Donde?" is a colloquial and humorous expression in Mexican Spanish. Here's a breakdown:

*   **"Verga"**: Literally means "rod" or "stick," but colloquially, it's a vulgar term for male genitalia. It's often used to express surprise, frustration, or emphasis, similar to how "fuck" is used in English.
*   **"¿Dónde vergas?"**: Translates to "Where the fuck?" It's an emphatic way of asking "Where?"

So, the phrase can be interpreted as a humorous or emphatic way of asking, "Where the hell are we going?" or "Where the fuck to?"

### **Step 1: Is 10171017 valid ASCII as-is?**

ASCII characters are typically encoded using:

*   **8 bits** (extended ASCII): values 0–255

from fpdf import FPDF

# Create a landscape PDF class
class LandscapePDF(FPDF):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(orientation='L', unit='mm', format='A4')
        self.set_auto_page_break(auto=True, margin=15)
        self.add_page()
        self.set_font("Arial", size=10)
    
    def header_section(self, title):
        self.set_font("Arial", "B", 14)
        self.cell(0, 10, title, ln=True, align="C")
        self.ln(4)
        self.set_font("Arial", size=10)
    
    def add_table_section(self, title, rows):
        self.set_font("Arial", "B", 12)
        self.cell(0, 10, title, ln=True)
        self.set_font("Arial", size=10)
        for row in rows:
            self.multi_cell(0, 8, f"- {row}", ln=True)
        self.ln(5)

# Adjusting encoding-related characters that caused errors in the previous PDF generation.
# Replacing curly quotes and other non-latin characters with safe ASCII alternatives.

def sanitize_text_list(text_list):
    return [text.replace("’", "'").replace("“", '"').replace("”", '"') for text in text_list]

# Sanitize all text content
zodiac_traits = sanitize_text_list(zodiac_traits)
vices_and_signals = sanitize_text_list(vices_and_signals)

### **2\. Section 9 - Eviction Notice Period (California)**

In California:

*   **30-Day Notice**: If tenant has lived there under 1 year
*   **60-Day Notice**: If over 1 year
*   **3-Day Notice**: If there's serious violation (e.g. non-payment, threats, illegal activity)

I’ll update Section 9 to reflect this:

**Viable, but here’s what to consider:**

*   You **own the property**, so that’s good.
*   You'll need to **check local zoning laws** in Cerritos or your county.
*   Most cities require a **short-term rental license**, insurance, and possibly an inspection.
*   A BnB or Airbnb could bring **3–4x more money per room** than long-term rent.

### **1\. One Subscription = One User Bucket**

Even if you break your project into different chats (say: one for Astrological, one for Sports, etc.), **they all pull from the same usage bucket**. That means:

*   **Same daily limit**
*   **Same message cap**
*   **Same data analysis threshold**

So even if you create 4 separate chat threads—one per class—**they still count toward your daily allowance** under your one ChatGPT Plus account. It’s like slicing a pizza four ways... you still only got one pizza.

### **2\. What Counts Against You?**

*   Every back-and-forth **message you send counts**.
*   **Longer messages or uploads that need processing** (like images or spreadsheets) chew more into your _compute limits_ (fancy term for backend CPU effort).
*   **Multimodal (image + text + analysis)**? You burn more than a “hi” message. So don’t waste a message on a damn emoji.

### **3\. Plus Plan vs Free Plan**

*   You’re using GPT-4-turbo (stronger, smarter, more memory).
*   You get **priority processing** in high traffic.
*   You have access to tools like **image uploads**, **file analysis**, **charting**, **coding**, **Python sandbox**, etc.

* * *

### TL;DR:

**YES**, all your chats—no matter how many—count under the same ChatGPT Plus limit.  
**NO**, you don’t get more usage just because you create more chat threads.  
**YES**, we can beat the system by batching messages smartly, offloading repeat work, and staying strategic.

# Define a function to calculate a custom gematria-style cipher with optional S substitution
def gematria_value(name, substitute_s=False):
    value = 0
    for char in name.upper():
        if char.isalpha():
            if substitute_s and char == 'S':
                value += 30
            else:
                value += ord(char) - ord('A') + 1
        elif char.isdigit():
            value += int(char)
    return value

    name = name.upper().replace(" ", "")

# Include custom substitution: S = 30 for SLIGHTLYSTQQPID

    return total
